- Fixed `BaseInputTemplate` passing its description to Mantine as a plain string, so it now renders through `DescriptionFieldTemplate` and honors `ui:enableMarkdownInDescription` like the other widgets
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Fixed Mantine widgets discarding `aria-describedby`: inputs are now described by the field's description, error and help ids. The Mantine peer dependency floor is now `>=9.6.3`. See the v7 upgrade guide ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Fixed `CheckboxesWidget` and `RadioWidget` labelling their group by a label id that was never rendered, and gave `RangeWidget`'s thumb and each `AltDateWidget` date part an accessible name. `RangeWidget` and `AltDateWidget` now render their title, description and errors in Mantine's `Input.Wrapper`. See the v7 upgrade guide ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed a Mantine widget's errors running together on one line ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed widgets other than `CheckboxWidget` ignoring `descriptionProps` from `ui:options` and the Mantine theme ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Fixed a `oneOf`/`anyOf` selector referencing no error and `CheckboxWidget` reading its error twice: Mantine's error element and `CheckboxWidget`'s now have the field's error id (`<id>__error`), and `ObjectFieldTemplate` renders the object's own errors. See the v7 upgrade guide ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed `RangeWidget` and `AltDateWidget` never marking their inputs invalid or rendering a `success` option ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed `AltDateTimeWidget` showing a zero hour, minute or second as empty, and `AltDateWidget` and `AltDateTimeWidget` leaving their Now and Clear buttons enabled when disabled or read-only and ignoring `autofocus`, `onBlur` and `onFocus` ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Fixed `RangeWidget`, `PasswordWidget`, `TimeWidget`, `ColorWidget`, `FileWidget` and `DateTimeInput` ignoring `nameGenerator`. They, `TextareaWidget` and `CheckboxWidget` now fall back to the field's id rather than the bare property name, as the other widgets do. See the v7 upgrade guide ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed `CheckboxWidget`, `PasswordWidget` and the options of `CheckboxesWidget` and `RadioWidget` never being marked invalid, the radios and `AltDateWidget`'s date parts never being marked required, and the checkbox and radio options not being described by a `success` message ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed a `oneOf`/`anyOf` field's errors showing twice, on its option's widget and under its selector; they show once ([#5332](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5332))
- Fixed `SelectWidget`, `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` with object options and the `realValue` option value format ([#5309](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5309))
- Fixed `realValue` options whose `String()` matches, such as `1` and `'1'`, sharing a DOM value ([#5315](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5315))
- Fixed `DateTimeInput` leaving a field blank for an epoch number or a `Date` when `ui:options` sets a custom `valueFormat`: text converted from a number or `Date` is ISO-ordered, so it is now parsed without `valueFormat` ([#5393](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5393))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `AltDateWidget`'s date parts are named the way every other theme names them, by the field's label and the translated part, such as `When, year`, through an `aria-label` rather than an `aria-labelledby` referencing the title, and their placeholders go through `translateString`. `SelectWidget` gives an `aria-label` it is passed to its input, and describes that input by an `aria-describedby` it is passed, through the `InputWrapper` context Mantine reads it from, in place of the field's ids. `AltDateWidget` no longer renders a hidden copy of a title the field doesn't show, which nothing refers to any more: `useFieldWrapperProps()` returns the `inputWrapperOrder` in its place, for `RangeWidget` to build one with `useHiddenTitle()` ([#5355](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5355), [#5357](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5357))
- Fixed `CheckboxesWidget` and `RadioWidget` rendering every option enabled and focusable on a `disabled` or `readonly` field, where only the options `ui:enumDisabled` lists were disabled ([#5487](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5487))
- `cleanupOptions()` strips the new `itemLabel` array option, so it isn't spread onto the Mantine component of an array rendered as a widget, such as checkboxes or a file input ([#5474](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5474))
- Fixed widgets spreading rjsf options `cleanupOptions()` didn't list onto their Mantine component: `ui:initialValue`, `ui:definitions`, a per-field override of a template such as `MarkdownTemplate`, the alt-date widgets' `yearsRange`, `format`, `hideNowButton` and `hideClearButton` on the inputs `DateWidget` and `DateTimeWidget` render, `ObjectFieldTemplate`'s `gridCols`, `gridSpacing` and `gridVerticalSpacing` from `ui:globalOptions`, and `required`, so a form-wide `ui:globalOptions.required`, which rjsf ignores, marked a boolean or array field rendered as a select, radio, checkboxes or file input required. The options it strips are now derived from `WidgetProps['options']` and `TemplatesType`, so an option or template added to either fails typecheck until it is listed. `TextareaWidget`, `FileWidget` and `ColorWidget` pass `ui:rows`, `ui:accept` and `ui:format` to their Mantine component themselves, `ui:format` unless it is one of the alt-date widgets' orders, such as `DMY` ([#5485](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5485))
- Declares its presentation: its widgets draw their own label and description, an `anyOf`/`oneOf` field is named and described by its option selector, and an object's template draws its errors. Its widgets draw the description `getWidgetDescription()` gives them, `SelectWidget` is described by nothing the plan doesn't render rather than by the default ids, and `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` labels the key through `hasAdditionalKeyLabel()` and aligns it with a description the value's widget draws ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldHelpTemplate` renders under the help id of its field's plan, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's own `ui:help` no longer repeats the field's id ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- Declares `FallbackField` as drawing its own label, so the fallback UI's value is labelled by its own widget, which no template label did before ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RangeWidget`'s thumb is described by the ids its field's plan renders, with no `aria-describedby` where nothing describes it, rather than by `ariaDescribedByIds()` of its own id, which an `anyOf`/`oneOf` selector rendering a range never renders ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` passes the plan's `aria-labelledby` to its input, so an object's option selector is named by the object's heading ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RangeWidget` puts the field's id on the slider's thumb, the element a user focuses, rather than on the slider's root, so `focusOnFirstError` reaches the thumb, where it found only the hidden input holding the value and focused nothing. A selector aimed at the slider's root by `#root_x` now matches the thumb; aim at `.mantine-Slider-root` instead. The field's id replaces an `id` given in `thumbProps`, since `focusOnFirstError` and the ids the field's plan references depend on it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- Declares `RangeWidget` as named through `aria-labelledby`, as `@rjsf/core` declares `AltDateWidget` and `AltDateTimeWidget`, so a label the template draws for one has no `for`, which named the slider's thumb, a `<div>` a `for` can't label. Every widget rendering a single input, and `CheckboxWidget`, takes the `aria-labelledby` its field's plan gives its control, so a widget declared `labelledBy: 'aria-labelledby'` is named by the template's label, where it had no name ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Declares `errors: 'self'` for its widgets and the `@rjsf/core` widgets it renders through its `BaseInputTemplate`, which draw the errors their field's plan gives them in Mantine's error slot. `FieldErrorTemplate` shows the errors the plan leaves to the template, in Mantine's error element under `errorId()`: those of a custom widget or `ui:field`, `@rjsf/core`'s `RatingWidget`, a field of an unsupported type, an array rendered item by item, which `ArrayFieldTemplate` no longer draws, and an `anyOf`/`oneOf` field, below its option rather than under its selector. It rendered a hidden copy of every field's errors instead, which took the field's error id only while no other element had it, so those errors were announced but never shown. A widget whose `inputWrapperOrder` leaves out `'error'` shows its errors through `FieldErrorTemplate` too, and a `CheckboxesWidget` with no options still draws its group, with the label, description and errors that say which field they belong to. A custom widget registered under one of the theme's widget keys takes that key's declaration and should draw `presentation.errors.messages` itself. A custom `ui:field` that hands one of this theme's widgets `rawErrors` but not its plan, as `FieldProps.presentation` advises for a field that doesn't declare `WIDGET_FIELD_PRESENTATION`, shows the field's errors twice: the widget, given no plan, draws them, and so does the template. Pass the widget `hideError` or no `rawErrors`, or declare `WIDGET_FIELD_PRESENTATION` for the field and hand the widget its `presentation`. ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The widgets put their field's plan on the element a user focuses: each input, each option of `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget`, `RangeWidget`'s thumb and each `AltDateWidget` date part is described only by the parts of its field that render, through `getControlDescribedBy()`, rather than by `ariaDescribedByIds()`, and marked invalid as the plan says, its error style read from `hasWidgetErrors()`, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's control is `aria-invalid`, and styled invalid, while its field's errors show, as its selector is. `CheckboxesWidget`, `RadioWidget` and `RangeWidget` are named by a label the plan has drawn elsewhere, as by the template for a widget declared `label: 'template'`, through the plan's `aria-labelledby`, and draw no hidden title of their own in its place. An input is named by the label text `ui:label: false` hides through the plan's `aria-label`, which it takes with the plan's `aria-labelledby`; a radio or checkboxes group, whose `radiogroup` or `group` element Mantine draws with only the `aria-labelledby` of its wrapper, is named by that text through the hidden title it keeps for it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` describes a `propertyNames` key select by nothing, rather than by its key's `__error`, `__description` and `__help` ids, which no element has ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` draws the field's label and description where its plan gives them the template, as for a custom widget declared `label: 'template'`: the label under the id its plan gives it, `fieldLabelId()`, or `optionLabelId()` beneath a label a field above draws, with the `for` that `templateLabelFor()` gives, which is none for a radio or checkboxes group named by it through `aria-labelledby`, marked required through `templateMarksRequired()`, so a widget declared `uncheckedIsFalse` is marked only where the schema demands `true`, and the description in Mantine's description element. It drew neither whatever the plan said, so such a field had none ([#5388](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5388), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `TitleField` and `ArrayFieldTitleTemplate` put the title id on a `<span>` around the title's text rather than on the `<h3>` or `<h4>`, and mark a required object's or array's heading `*` outside that `<span>`, as `@rjsf/core`'s do, so an object's option selector named by that id is named "Pet" rather than "Pet *". A selector or style targeting `h3#…__title` or `h4#…__title` now targets the `<span>` inside the heading ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `ArrayFieldTemplate` draws only the heading `ArrayField` hands it as `title`, the one the array draws, as `@rjsf/core`'s does, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option repeating its field's `ui:title` no longer draws it a second time, and it and `ObjectFieldTemplate` lay out an optional array's or object's data control with its items or properties where they draw no heading, as under `ui:label: false`, rather than dropping it with the heading, reading that rule through `titleDrawsOptionalDataControl()`. `ArrayFieldTitleTemplate` draws its heading wherever it is handed a `title`, no longer re-reading `ui:label`, which disagreed with the field's where `ui:globalOptions` turns labels off and the field's `ui:label` turns them on, and so dropped the heading and the data control handed to it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldHelpTemplate` renders the help in a `<div>` rather than a `<p>`, since `RichHelp` can render markdown blocks, which a `<p>` can't hold ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- Declares `@rjsf/core`'s `RatingWidget`, which draws no label or description, as leaving both to the template, so `FieldTemplate` draws them and its stars' `radiogroup` is named by the label, where a rating field had neither and was described by a description id no element had. A widget of your own registered as `RatingWidget` takes that declaration, so one that draws its own label should be declared `label: 'self'` under that key, and one that draws its own description `description: 'self'`, as the upgrade guide's "Group labels" entry says ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RadioWidget` focuses its checked radio under `ui:autofocus`, where Tab into the group lands, or the first a user can choose where none is checked or `ui:enumDisabled` disables it, and `CheckboxesWidget` the first checkbox a user can choose, where both took the first option, which takes no focus where `ui:enumDisabled` disables it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` names an `additionalProperties` entry's key input by the key's label through `aria-label` where `ui:label: false` leaves no label drawn, as `@rjsf/core`'s does, and a `multiple` `SelectWidget`'s search input is `aria-required` while its field is required, since Mantine's `MultiSelect` puts `required` on its hidden input alone ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `cleanupOptions()` no longer lists `definitions` and `globalOptions`, which `getUiOptions()` no longer returns ([#5527](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5527))
- Declares where its widgets draw their own label: above the control, `labelPlacement: 'above'`, for every widget, the `anyOf`/`oneOf` option selector and the fallback UI's type selector, and beside it, `'beside'`, for the checkbox, so a custom `ArrayFieldItemTemplate` is handed `displayLabel: true` for an item whose widget draws its label above it
- The theme declares `gridCols`, `gridSpacing` and `gridVerticalSpacing` as its `globalUiOptionsKeys`, typed by the new `MantineGlobalUISchemaOptions` and the new `UiSchema` type, which takes them inline, so global ones reach `ObjectFieldTemplate` outside a `LayoutGridField` too ([#5607](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5607))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** The widgets leave every key the registry's `globalUiOptions` hold out of the props they hand their Mantine component, unless the field sets it in its own options, so an extension key a form declares for its own components no longer reaches every Mantine input, and a Mantine prop such as `radius` or `variant` set in `ui:globalOptions`, which reached the widgets of `BooleanField` and `ArrayField`, is set in each field's `ui:options` instead. `cleanupOptions()` takes the widget's `registry` and `uiSchema` as its second parameter, ahead of the extra keys to leave out. `MantineGlobalUISchemaOptions` rejects a user extension named `wrapperProps`, `thumbProps`, `thumbLabel`, `valueFormat`, `displayFormat` or one of the `InputWrapper` options, such as `size`, `labelProps` or `inputContainer`, which the widgets read from their options ([#5607](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5607))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** A single `SelectWidget` whose schema has a `default` no longer clears its value when its selected option is clicked again, as the other themes' leave out their empty option under a default, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` field's option selector can't be cleared to no option and drop the field's data. Set `allowDeselect` in the field's `ui:options` to keep it ([#5527](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5527))

## @rjsf/mui

- Updated the README, which said "Material UI 7 requires React 18, so you will need to upgrade" — the peer requirement is now React 19
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Dropped support for `@mui/material`/`@mui/icons-material` version 7; the peer dependency range is now `^9.0.0`
- `BaseInputTemplate` forwards the `inputMode` and `pattern` from `getInputProps()` to the native `<input>` through `slotProps.htmlInput`, so a `number`/`integer` field gets the numeric text input like `@rjsf/core`; the `getInputProps()` attributes are now applied first, so a `slotProps.htmlInput` or `ui:options.mui.slotProps.htmlInput` value always takes precedence, and `BaseInputTemplateMuiProps['slotProps']['htmlInput']` now accepts the `<input>`-only attributes such as `pattern`, `step`, `min`, `max` and `accept` ([#4038](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4038))
- Added `<optgroup>`-equivalent support to `SelectWidget` via `ui:options.optgroups`, using `ListSubheader`
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` renders an additional property's key as a `SelectWidget` of the names the parent schema's `propertyNames.enum` allows, instead of a free-text input, and takes the key from the new `keyName` prop rather than from the decorated `label`, so a `deprecated` additional property no longer offers its decoration as a key name, nor renames itself to it ([#4682](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4682))
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` sizes the key dropdown to its column, which the free-text key input it replaces already did through `fullWidth`; a bare mui `TextField` renders at intrinsic width ([#4682](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4682))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` deriving their `error` prop from `rawErrors` alone, so a field kept its `Mui-error` styling under `ui:hideError` ([#3684](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/3684))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** The default `Form` export is a `ThemedForm` (see `@rjsf/core`): generic over the form data, so `<Form formData={data} />` infers `T` and `<Form<MyData> />` names it. The `Theme`, `Templates` and `Widgets` exports keep each component's own generic type instead of being instantiated at the default `T` (`any` in 6.x), so `withTheme<MyData>(Theme)` and `{ ...Templates, FieldTemplate: MyFieldTemplate }` typecheck for any `T`. `generateForm()` returns a `ThemedForm<T, S, F>`; `generateTheme()`, `generateTemplates()` and `generateWidgets()` keep their signatures, apart from the new `unknown`/`FormContextType` defaults, and still build a fresh object on every call
- Fixed object options in `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget`, and `SelectWidget` reports its form data value on focus and blur ([#5309](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5309), [#5317](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5317))
- Fixed a theme callback given as `sx` in a `ui:options.mui.rjsfSlotProps` target (e.g. `titleBox`, `errorBox`) being silently dropped: it is now applied, composed after the theme's default styles. An array or callback passed as the default `sx` to `computeSxProps()` (deep-importable from `@rjsf/mui/lib/util.js`) is composed the same way instead of being spread into an object ([#5386](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5386))
- Fixed `realValue` options whose `String()` matches, such as `1` and `'1'`, sharing a DOM value; `CheckboxesWidget` reports the focused option on focus and blur ([#5315](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5315))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `AltDateWidget`'s date parts having no accessible name: each select is named by the field's label and the translated part, such as `When, year`, even when the label is hidden, and described by the field's description, help and errors. `SelectWidget` gives the `aria-label` and `aria-describedby` it is passed, or its own `aria-describedby`, to its combobox rather than the field's root, through `widgetAriaProps()`. An `htmlInput` given through `rjsfSlotProps`, which is now typed and may be MUI's callback form, is applied over them, so an `aria-label` or `aria-describedby` set there is kept ([#5355](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5355), [#5357](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5357))
- Declares its presentation: its widgets draw their own label, its checkbox its description too, and an `anyOf`/`oneOf` field is named by its option selector. `FieldTemplate` renders the description it is handed under a plan, whatever `displayLabel` says, and without one, through `templateDrawsDescription()`, only where `displayLabel` lets it draw the label, as before the plan, `CheckboxWidget` draws the description `getWidgetDescription()` gives it and is described by the ids its plan renders, and `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` labels the key through `hasAdditionalKeyLabel()` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldHelpTemplate` renders under the help id of its field's plan, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's own `ui:help` no longer repeats the field's id ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- Declares `FallbackField` as drawing its own label, so the fallback UI's value is labelled by its own widget, which no template label did before ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` hands the plan's `aria-labelledby` to MUI's select as its `labelId`, the only way MUI names its combobox, so an object's option selector is named by the object's heading ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` take their error state from `hasWidgetErrors()`, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's control is styled invalid while the field above draws its errors ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The widgets put their field's plan on the element a user focuses, through `getControlAttributes()`, rather than on the MUI wrapper around it, so each is `aria-invalid` while its errors show and described only by the elements that render, rather than by `ariaDescribedByIds()`: `BaseInputTemplate`, and so `TextareaWidget`, on its `<input>` or `<textarea>` through `slotProps.htmlInput` rather than the `TextField`'s root `<div>`, `CheckboxWidget` on its `<input>` rather than the `<span>` around it, and `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` on each option's `<input>` through `getGroupAttributes()`, with `RadioWidget`'s `radiogroup` rather than each radio `aria-invalid`. A slot the form author gives the control, through `rjsfSlotProps` or `BaseInputTemplate`'s `slotProps.htmlInput`, is applied over them, as `SelectWidget`'s `htmlInput` is. `RadioWidget` takes `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored, as core's does, focusing its checked radio, where Tab into the group lands, or the first a user can choose where none is checked or `ui:enumDisabled` disables it, and `CheckboxesWidget` the first checkbox a user can choose, where it took the first, which takes no focus where `ui:enumDisabled` disables it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RangeWidget` puts the field's id and plan on its slider's `<input>`, the element a user focuses, rather than on the slider's root `<span>`, so its label's `for` names the slider and `focusOnFirstError` reaches it. The `<input>` is `required` for a required field, as core's is, takes `htmlName` as its `name`, as the other widgets' inputs do, where its `name` was the field's id, and is focused under `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored. A slider of several values, whose every thumb `Slider` gives the `input` slot, keeps the field's id on its root, which only one element may carry and a label's `for` can't name, so its label names every thumb through `aria-labelledby` instead, and its first thumb is focused under `ui:autofocus`. The slider's root no longer carries the field's label as a `label` attribute, which `Slider` doesn't take. A selector aimed at `span#root_x` should aim at `input#root_x` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` name their group by the label they draw, under the id their plan gives it, `fieldLabelId()`, or `optionLabelId()` beneath a label a field above draws, through `aria-labelledby`, and draw that label with no `for`, which named the group's wrapper, an element a `for` can't label, so neither group had a name. `CheckboxesWidget`'s group takes the `group` role, and a group whose label `ui:label: false` hides is named by that label's text, the `aria-label` its plan gives it. Each radio of a required `RadioWidget` is `required`, as core's are. A selector aimed at `label[for="root_color"]` for such a field should aim at `#root_color__label` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` add the helper text MUI's `TextField` renders for a `helperText`, as in `ui:options.mui.helperText`, to their control's `aria-describedby`, which replaced the one MUI links it by, so the helper text is announced beside the field's description, help and errors. `BaseInputTemplate` applies a `slotProps.htmlInput` given as MUI's `(ownerState) => props`, which it dropped, and `BaseInputTemplateMuiProps['slotProps']['htmlInput']` accepts that form ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` draws the field's label where its plan gives the template the label, as for a custom widget declared `label: 'template'`, under the id its plan gives it, `fieldLabelId()`, or `optionLabelId()` beneath a label a field above draws, with the `for` that `templateLabelFor()` gives, which is none for a radio or checkboxes group named by it through `aria-labelledby`, and marks it required through `templateMarksRequired()`, so a widget declared `uncheckedIsFalse` is marked only where the schema demands `true`. It drew no label whatever the plan said, so such a field had none. The theme declares `@rjsf/core`'s `RatingWidget`, `AltDateWidget` and `AltDateTimeWidget` `label: 'template'`, since none of them draws a label, so those fields are labelled by the template, a rating's `radiogroup` named by that label and the date parts' label drawn with no `for`, which would name no element, where under `default: { label: 'self' }` they had no label at all. A widget of your own registered under one of those keys takes that declaration, so one that draws its own label, such as a MUI `Rating` registered as `RatingWidget` or a date picker registered as `AltDateWidget`, should be declared `label: 'self'` under its key, as the upgrade guide's "Group labels" entry says. `SelectWidget` is declared `labelledBy: 'aria-labelledby'`, since MUI names its combobox only through its `labelId`, which a `for` can't reach, so a `SelectWidget` declared `label: 'template'` is named by the template's label rather than by nothing ([#5388](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5388), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `TitleField` puts the `titleId()` it is handed on a `<span>` around the title's text rather than on the `Box` around the heading, its optional data control and its divider, and marks a required object's or array's heading `*` outside that `<span>`, as core's does, so an object's option selector named by that id is named "Pet" rather than "Pet Remove data for optional field". A selector or style targeting `#…__title` now targets the `<span>` inside the heading ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `ArrayFieldTemplate` draws only the heading `ArrayField` hands it as `title`, the one the array draws, as core's does, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option repeating its field's `ui:title` no longer draws it a second time, and lays out an optional array's data control with its items where it draws no heading, as under `ui:label: false`, rather than dropping it with the heading ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `ObjectFieldTemplate` lays out the optional data control with the properties where it draws no heading, as under `ui:label: false`, rather than dropping it with the heading, as core's does ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `MultiSchemaFieldTemplate` lays out no `FormControl` for a selector it isn't given, as while an optional `anyOf`/`oneOf` field has no data, rather than an empty one ([#5401](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5401), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `FieldErrorTemplate` puts `errorId()` on its list alone, the one element a control is described by, and no longer gives each message an id of its own (`root_x__error-0`, `root_x__error-1`, …). A selector aimed at one message should aim at the list's items instead ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` apply the props a form author gives their group, through `ui:options.mui` or `rjsfSlotProps.radioGroup` and `rjsfSlotProps.formGroup`, over their group's `role`, `aria-labelledby`, `aria-label` and `aria-invalid` from the plan, as each radio's and checkbox's slot is applied over the plan's control attributes, where the plan replaced them. A `required` in `rjsfSlotProps.radio` or `rjsfSlotProps.checkbox` goes on each radio's or checkbox's `<input>`, as the field's does, rather than marking every option's label `*`, and replaces the field's, so `required: false` turns a required field's radios off. An `autoFocus` in either is applied as the author gives it, where the widget's own `ui:autofocus` choice replaced it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` draw no label for a required field with no label text, which would hold only its `*`, where the old `<FormLabel required>` drew one; the radios stay `required`. The date parts of `@rjsf/core`'s `AltDateWidget`, which this theme renders through its `SelectWidget`, are drawn in the error state while the field's errors show, since each part's plan marks it `aria-invalid`, where they never were ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490), [#5542](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5542))
- `SelectWidget`'s combobox, where no label names it, as for an untitled root `anyOf`/`oneOf` selector, is named by the field's label, its placeholder or the translated select prompt through `getControlFallbackName()`, as `@rjsf/daisyui`'s, `@rjsf/fluentui-rc`'s and `@rjsf/chakra-ui`'s are, rather than by nothing. `CheckboxWidget` falls back to the schema `description` for an empty `ui:description`, as every field's description does, where it used to hide it. `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate`'s free-text key input, where `ui:label: false` leaves the key unlabelled, is named by the key's label through `aria-label`, as core's is, rather than by nothing ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- Declares the labels its radio and checkbox groups and its slider draw above the control `labelPlacement: 'above'`, so a custom `ArrayFieldItemTemplate` is handed `displayLabel: true` for an item holding one; a text input or select draws its label inside its outline, and its item is handed `false`
- The theme declares `mui` as its `globalUiOptionsKeys`, typed by the new `MuiGlobalUISchemaOptions` and the new `UiSchema` type, which takes it inline, so a global `mui` reaches every widget, a custom widget's included, and every template but the form-level `ErrorList` and `SubmitButton`, outside a `LayoutGridField` too ([#5607](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5607))

## @rjsf/react-bootstrap

- Fixed `lib/index.js` being unloadable by Node: it imported `react-bootstrap/Col`-style directory subpaths and extensionless `@react-icons/all-files` paths, which only bundlers resolve. Components are now imported from `react-bootstrap` itself and icon files by their full `.js` name ([#5244](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5244))
- Fixed the `getTestRegistry()` test helper passing an options object where positional arguments were expected, which silently dropped its templates and widgets; it now imports from `@rjsf/core/testing` ([#5272](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5272))
- `BaseInputTemplate` renders a `number`/`integer` field as `<input type="text" inputMode="decimal|numeric" pattern="..." title="...">` instead of `<input type="number">`, and no longer puts `step`, `min` or `max` on it, so the rendered markup and any snapshot of a numeric field change; see the `getInputProps()` entry under `@rjsf/utils` for the reasoning and the ways back to a native number input. A `pattern` or `inputMode` passed through `extraProps` still wins over the derived one, as it did when neither was derived, and replacing the `pattern` drops the derived `title` with it rather than leaving it to describe a rule no longer in force ([#4038](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4038))
- Added `<optgroup>` support to `SelectWidget` via `ui:options.optgroups`. Grouping only affects how the options are displayed: a multiple select still reports its values in enum order, so adding `optgroups` never changes the order of the array written to form data
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` renders an additional property's key as a `SelectWidget` of the names the parent schema's `propertyNames.enum` allows, instead of a free-text input, and takes the key from the new `keyName` prop rather than from the decorated `label`, so a `deprecated` additional property no longer offers its decoration as a key name, nor renames itself to it ([#4682](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4682))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` applying `is-invalid` from `rawErrors` alone, so a field stayed outlined as invalid under `ui:hideError` ([#3684](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/3684))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** The default `Form` export is a `ThemedForm` (see `@rjsf/core`): generic over the form data, so `<Form formData={data} />` infers `T` and `<Form<MyData> />` names it. The `Theme`, `Templates` and `Widgets` exports keep each component's own generic type instead of being instantiated at the default `T` (`any` in 6.x), so `withTheme<MyData>(Theme)` and `{ ...Templates, FieldTemplate: MyFieldTemplate }` typecheck for any `T`. `generateForm()` returns a `ThemedForm<T, S, F>`; `generateTheme()`, `generateTemplates()` and `generateWidgets()` keep their signatures, apart from the new `unknown`/`FormContextType` defaults, and still build a fresh object on every call
- Fixed object options in `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` ([#5317](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5317))
- Fixed `realValue` options whose `String()` matches, such as `1` and `'1'`, sharing a DOM value; `CheckboxesWidget` reports the focused option on focus and blur; `SelectWidget` options no longer render an `id`, as in the other themes ([#5315](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5315))
- `TextareaWidget` no longer replaces a `ui:options.rows` of `0` with the default of 5 rows. React leaves a `rows` of `0` off the textarea, so it gets the browser's default height of 2 rows, as with `@rjsf/core`'s `TextareaWidget` ([#5387](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5387))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `AltDateWidget`'s date parts having no accessible name: each select is named by the field's label and the translated part, such as `When, year`, even when the label is hidden, and described by the field's description, help and errors. `SelectWidget` gives the `aria-label` and `aria-describedby` it is passed to its `<select>` through `widgetAriaProps()` ([#5355](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5355), [#5357](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5357))
- `FieldTemplate` renders the description it is handed under a plan, whatever `displayLabel` says, and without one, through `templateDrawsDescription()`, only where `displayLabel` lets it draw the label, as before the plan, `CheckboxWidget` draws the description `getWidgetDescription()` gives it and is described by the ids its plan renders, and `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` labels the key through `hasAdditionalKeyLabel()` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` puts the label id its plan gives it, `fieldLabelId()`, or `optionLabelId()` beneath a label a field above draws, on the label's text, which an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option selector is named by ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldHelpTemplate` renders under the help id of its field's plan, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's own `ui:help` no longer repeats the field's id ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` hands `SelectedOptionDescription` its `hideLabel` and plan, so the selected option's description renders under the id the select is described by ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` take their error state from `hasWidgetErrors()`, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's control is styled invalid while the field above draws its errors ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The widgets put their field's plan on the element a user focuses, through `getControlAttributes()`, so each is `aria-invalid` while its errors show and described only by the elements that render, rather than by `ariaDescribedByIds()`: `BaseInputTemplate`, `TextareaWidget` and `RangeWidget` on their control, and `CheckboxWidget` on its `<input>` rather than the `Form.Group` around it, which took its `aria-describedby` while the `<input>` carrying its id took none. `RangeWidget` renders its input through `BaseInputTemplate`, as core's does, so it takes `htmlName` as its `name`, where its `name` was the field's id, is focused under `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored, and is offered the schema's `examples`, as the other inputs are. It carries `required` for a required field, as core's does, though a browser doesn't enforce `required` on a range input. A read-only range is disabled, as core's is, since a browser ignores `readonly` on a range input and let a user drag it, and `RangeWidget` draws no `allowClearTextInputs` clear button, which would empty a value the slider can't show. `BaseInputTemplate` renders any `range` input, such as one from `ui:options.inputType`, as react-bootstrap's `Form.Range`, which gives it Bootstrap's `form-range` class rather than `form-control`, under the prefix a `ThemeProvider` gives `form-range`. `RadioWidget` takes `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored, as core's does, focusing its checked radio, where Tab into the group lands, or the first a user can choose where none is checked or `ui:enumDisabled` disables it, and `CheckboxesWidget` the first checkbox a user can choose, where it took the first, which takes no focus where `ui:enumDisabled` disables it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` name their group by the template's label through `getGroupAttributes()`, which its `for` can't reach, so neither group had a name. The group's `Form.Group` carries the field's id and the `radiogroup` or `group` role, `RadioWidget`'s `radiogroup` rather than each radio is `aria-invalid`, each of `CheckboxesWidget`'s checkboxes is, and each option is described by the field's errors, description and help only where they render. `FieldTemplate` draws the label with the `for` that `templateLabelFor()` gives, as core's does, which is none for such a group, nor for `@rjsf/core`'s `RatingWidget`, `AltDateWidget` and `AltDateTimeWidget`. A selector aimed at `label[for="root_color"]` for such a field should aim at `label:has(#root_color__label)`, the label holding the `*`, or `label #root_color__label` for its text alone ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `CheckboxesWidget` no longer marks each of a required field's checkboxes `required`, which made the browser block the submit until every option was checked, as core's dropped it ([#1194](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/1194))
- `TextareaWidget`, `RangeWidget`, `CheckboxWidget`, `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` style their controls invalid while their errors show, as `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` do, reading the same `hasWidgetErrors()`, so a field announced `aria-invalid` also looks it and none does under `ui:hideError`: every control takes Bootstrap's `is-invalid` class through react-bootstrap's `isInvalid`, which on a checkbox or radio also colours its option's label, `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` included, and a range input, since `Form.Range` takes no `isInvalid`, takes it beside its own `className`, so a `className` handed to `BaseInputTemplate` through `extraProps` no longer drops it. Bootstrap draws no invalid style for `.form-range`, so a range field shows its error state through the template's red label alone unless your own CSS styles `.form-range.is-invalid` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` marks its label required from the plan's `label.requiredMarker` through `templateMarksRequired()`, falling back to `required` without a plan, so a label it draws for a widget declared `uncheckedIsFalse` is marked only where the schema demands `true` ([#5388](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5388), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `TitleField` puts the `titleId()` it is handed on a `<span>` around the title's text rather than on the `<div>` around the heading, its optional data control and its divider, draws the `title` it is handed rather than `ui:title` over it, and marks a required object's or array's heading `*` outside that `<span>`, as core's does, so an object's option selector named by that id is named "Pet" rather than "Pet Remove data for optional field". A selector aimed at `div#root__title` should aim at `span#root__title` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `ArrayFieldTemplate` draws only the heading `ArrayField` hands it as `title`, the one the array draws, as core's does, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option repeating its field's `ui:title` no longer draws it a second time. It and `ObjectFieldTemplate` lay out an optional array's or object's data control with its items or properties where they draw no heading, as under `ui:label: false`, rather than dropping it with the heading, reading that rule through `titleDrawsOptionalDataControl()` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `MultiSchemaFieldTemplate` lays out no `Card.Body` for a selector it isn't given, as while an optional `anyOf`/`oneOf` field has no data, rather than an empty one ([#5401](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5401), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `FieldHelpTemplate` and `FieldTemplate`'s description render their `Form.Text` as a `<div>` rather than a `<small>`, which can't hold the blocks `RichHelp` and `RichDescription` render, such as markdown paragraphs. A selector or style aimed at `small#root__help` should aim at `div#root__help` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The date parts of `@rjsf/core`'s `AltDateWidget`, which this theme renders through its `SelectWidget`, are drawn in the error state while the field's errors show, since each part's plan marks it `aria-invalid`, where they never were ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490), [#5542](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5542))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `FieldTemplate`'s label puts its plan's label id on a `<span>` around the label's text, as `@rjsf/core`'s does, so a group or an option selector named through it, such as a required radio group, is named by the text without the `*`; a selector aimed at `label#root_color__label` should aim at `label #root_color__label`. `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` names an `additionalProperties` entry's key input by the key's label through `aria-label` where `ui:label: false` leaves no label drawn ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))

## @rjsf/shadcn

- Converted the internal `forwardRef`-wrapped `Command` and `CommandInput` components to plain function components that accept `ref` as a regular prop, now that React 19 supports this natively
- Fixed the `getTestRegistry()` test helper passing an options object where positional arguments were expected, which silently dropped its templates and widgets; it now imports from `@rjsf/core/testing` ([#5272](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5272))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Stopped shipping compiled stylesheets. The package styled itself through ten full Tailwind builds under `src/css`, each compiled by a `build:css` script into a 37KB stylesheet carrying preflight, which restyled the rest of the consumer's page. `src/css`, `build-css.ts`, the `build:css` script and the `tailwindcss` dependency are all gone; the package now ships only its source, and a consumer's own Tailwind build scans it with `@source` and supplies the design tokens, which is how a Tailwind library is meant to be consumed and how shadcn itself expects the application to own its theme. The ten themes live on as token files in the playground for anyone who wants to copy one ([#5287](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5287))
- `BaseInputTemplate` renders a `number`/`integer` field as `<input type="text" inputMode="decimal|numeric" pattern="..." title="...">` instead of `<input type="number">`, and no longer puts `step`, `min` or `max` on it, so the rendered markup and any snapshot of a numeric field change; see the `getInputProps()` entry under `@rjsf/utils` for the reasoning and the ways back to a native number input. A `pattern` or `inputMode` passed through `extraProps` still wins over the derived one, as it did when neither was derived, and replacing the `pattern` drops the derived `title` with it rather than leaving it to describe a rule no longer in force ([#4038](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4038))
- Added `ui:options.optgroups` support to `SelectWidget`, rendering one labeled `CommandGroup` per group in `FancySelect`/`FancyMultiSelect`. Grouping only affects how the options are displayed: the order a multi-select reports its values in is independent of the grouping, so adding, reordering or removing a group never changes the order of the array written to form data
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` renders an additional property's key as a `SelectWidget` of the names the parent schema's `propertyNames.enum` allows, instead of a free-text input, and takes the key from the new `keyName` prop rather than from the decorated `label`, so a `deprecated` additional property no longer offers its decoration as a key name, nor renames itself to it ([#4682](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4682))
- `SelectWidget` gives the button that opens its dropdown the `id` it is passed, so a label pointing at it reaches the control ([#4682](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/4682))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` applying `border-destructive` from `rawErrors` alone, so a field stayed outlined as invalid under `ui:hideError` ([#3684](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/3684))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** The default `Form` export is a `ThemedForm` (see `@rjsf/core`): generic over the form data, so `<Form formData={data} />` infers `T` and `<Form<MyData> />` names it. The `Theme`, `Templates` and `Widgets` exports keep each component's own generic type instead of being instantiated at the default `T` (`any` in 6.x), so `withTheme<MyData>(Theme)` and `{ ...Templates, FieldTemplate: MyFieldTemplate }` typecheck for any `T`. `generateForm()` returns a `ThemedForm<T, S, F>`; `generateTheme()`, `generateTemplates()` and `generateWidgets()` keep their signatures, apart from the new `unknown`/`FormContextType` defaults, and still build a fresh object on every call
- Fixed the multiple `SelectWidget` with object options and the `realValue` option value format, and the single one's focus and blur value ([#5309](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5309))
- Fixed `realValue` options whose `String()` matches, such as `1` and `'1'`, sharing a DOM value; `CheckboxesWidget` reports the focused option on focus and blur ([#5315](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5315))
- `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` no longer interpolates an absent `classNames` into its class attribute, which produced a literal `undefined` class on an additional property rendered by a custom field that passes none ([#5327](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5327))
- `TextareaWidget` no longer replaces a `ui:options.rows` of `0` with the default of 5 rows. React leaves a `rows` of `0` off the textarea, so it gets the browser's default height of 2 rows, as with `@rjsf/core`'s `TextareaWidget`, or the `Textarea`'s `min-h-16` minimum height when that's taller ([#5387](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5387))
- Fixed the multiple `SelectWidget` rendering its cmdk search combobox with no accessible name, fixing [#5458](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5458): the field's resolved label (the schema `title` or a `ui:title` override, falling back to `ui:placeholder` and then the new `TranslatableString.SelectPlaceholder` for untitled fields) is passed through `FancyMultiSelect` to `Command`'s `label`, the same `ui:placeholder`/translated fallback fills the search input's visible placeholder, and the field's description and `aria-required` now reach the focusable combobox input; the single `SelectWidget` fills an empty trigger from the same fallback, so a single select without `ui:placeholder` now shows the translated placeholder where it used to render blank; the `placeholder` prop of the deep-imported `FancySelect` is now required and `FancyMultiSelect`'s `ariaPlaceholder` prop is removed, both type-level breaks for anyone building a custom widget on them
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `AltDateWidget`'s date parts having no accessible name: each select is named by the field's label and the translated part, such as `When, year`, even when the label is hidden, and described by the field's description, help and errors. `SelectWidget` gives the `aria-label` and `aria-describedby` it is passed to its trigger, or to its search input when `multiple`, through `widgetAriaProps()` ([#5355](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5355), [#5357](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5357))
- `FieldTemplate` renders the description it is handed under a plan, whatever `displayLabel` says, and without one, through `templateDrawsDescription()`, only where `displayLabel` lets it draw the label, as before the plan, `CheckboxWidget` draws the description `getWidgetDescription()` gives it and is described by the ids its plan renders, `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` labels the key through `hasAdditionalKeyLabel()`, and the multi-select is named by a label its plan says the template draws ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldHelpTemplate` renders under the help id of its field's plan, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's own `ui:help` no longer repeats the field's id ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate`'s label carries the id its plan gives it, `fieldLabelId()`, or `optionLabelId()` beneath a label a field above draws, and `SelectWidget` gives its button the `aria-labelledby` of its plan, through `FancySelect`'s new `ariaLabelledby`, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option selector is named by its field's label rather than by the option it displays ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `CheckboxWidget` carries the native `required` attribute only where its schema demands `true`, so a required `boolean` left unchecked submits as `false` ([#5388](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5388), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` hands `SelectedOptionDescription` its `hideLabel` and plan, so the selected option's description renders under the id the select is described by ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` take their error state from `hasWidgetErrors()`, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option's control is styled invalid while the field above draws its errors ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The widgets put their field's plan on the element a user focuses, through `getControlAttributes()`, so each is `aria-invalid` while its errors show and described only by the elements that render, rather than by `ariaDescribedByIds()`: `BaseInputTemplate` and `TextareaWidget` on their control, and `CheckboxWidget` on its checkbox rather than the `<div>` around it, which took its `aria-describedby` while the checkbox carrying its id took none ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RangeWidget` gives the field's id and plan to its slider's thumb, the element a user focuses, rather than to the slider's root, which took its `aria-describedby` while the thumb had no name. The theme declares `RangeWidget` `labelledBy: 'aria-labelledby'`, since a `for` can't reach the thumb, so the thumb is named by the template's label through `aria-labelledby` and `FieldTemplate` draws a range's label with no `for`. `RangeWidget` takes `htmlName` as the `name` its form value is submitted under, where it gave it no name, and focuses its thumb under `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored, and the `Slider` component passes its new `thumbProps` to each thumb, their `id` and `ref` to the first alone, so a range of several values, which `ui:options.props.value` can give, carries the field's id once. A selector aimed at the slider's root through `#root_volume` now finds its thumb, and one aimed at `label[for="root_volume"]` should aim at `#root_volume__label` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` name their group by the template's label through `getGroupAttributes()`, which its `for` can't reach, so neither group had a name. The group's wrapper carries the field's id and the `radiogroup` or `group` role, `RadioWidget`'s `radiogroup` rather than each radio is `aria-invalid`, each of `CheckboxesWidget`'s checkboxes is, and each option is described by the field's errors, description and help only where they render, rather than the `radiogroup` alone. `FieldTemplate` draws the label with the `for` that `templateLabelFor()` gives, as core's does, which is none for such a group. `RadioWidget` takes `ui:autofocus`, which it ignored, as core's does, focusing its checked radio, where Tab into the group lands, or the first a user can choose where none is checked or `ui:enumDisabled` disables it, and `CheckboxesWidget` the first checkbox a user can choose, where it took the first, which takes no focus where `ui:enumDisabled` disables it. A selector aimed at `label[for="root_color"]` for such a field should aim at `#root_color__label` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `CheckboxesWidget` no longer marks each of a required field's checkboxes `required`, which made the browser block the submit until every option was checked, as core's dropped it ([#1194](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/1194))
- `TextareaWidget`, `RangeWidget`, `CheckboxWidget`, `RadioWidget` and `CheckboxesWidget` style their controls invalid while their errors show, as `BaseInputTemplate` and `SelectWidget` do, reading the same `hasWidgetErrors()`, so a field announced `aria-invalid` also looks it and none does under `ui:hideError`: each control takes `border-destructive`, a range's thumb included ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RangeWidget` calls `onFocus` and `onBlur` as its thumb takes and loses focus, as every other theme's range does, where it called neither ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RadioWidget` takes `htmlName` as the `name` its form value is submitted under, as `CheckboxesWidget` does, where it gave its radios no name, so a native submit left the field out ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RangeWidget` draws an empty range's thumb at its minimum, as a browser draws a thumb on an empty native range, where Radix hid it, which left a required range with no value nothing to focus by keyboard, `ui:autofocus` or `focusOnFirstError`. It doesn't give the form that value by drawing it: it calls no `onChange` until a press on the slider or a key that moves its thumb leaves the thumb at its minimum, which Radix reports no change for, and then commits the minimum, and it names no form value until it has one, since Radix's hidden input would submit the minimum ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RadioWidget` calls `onFocus` and `onBlur` as each radio takes and loses focus, as core's does, rather than as its group does, which takes focus itself to pass it on to a radio, as when it is tabbed into or `focusOnFirstError` focuses the field's id it carries, and so reported a focus and a blur of no value first ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `SelectWidget`'s single-select trigger is a `combobox`, as `@rjsf/daisyui`'s is, so it is `aria-invalid` while its errors show and `aria-required` where its field is required, which a button can't be, and holds the option it displays as its value rather than being described by it. With no label drawn for it, it is named by the field's label, else by its placeholder, or, for a field with neither, by the translated select prompt, since a combobox takes no name from what it displays. The multiple select's search input is `aria-invalid` too. `FancySelect` loses its `named` prop and takes `ariaInvalid`, as `FancyMultiSelect` does. A query for the trigger by the `button` role should use `combobox` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget` focuses its single select's trigger, or its multiple select's search input, under `ui:autofocus`, which `FancySelect` and `FancyMultiSelect` gave to the `<div>` around it, which React doesn't focus ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` marks its label required from the plan's `label.requiredMarker` through `templateMarksRequired()`, falling back to `required` without a plan, so a label it draws for a widget declared `uncheckedIsFalse` is marked only where the schema demands `true` ([#5388](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5388), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `TitleField` puts the `titleId()` it is handed on a `<span>` around the title's text rather than on the `<div>` around the heading, its optional data control and its separator, draws the `title` it is handed rather than `ui:title` over it, and marks a required object's or array's heading `*` outside that `<span>`, as core's does, so an object's option selector named by that id is named "Pet" rather than "Pet Remove data for optional field". A selector aimed at `div#root__title` should aim at `span#root__title` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `ArrayFieldTemplate` draws only the heading `ArrayField` hands it as `title`, the one the array draws, as core's does, so an `anyOf`/`oneOf` option repeating its field's `ui:title` no longer draws it a second time. It and `ObjectFieldTemplate` lay out an optional array's or object's data control with its items or properties where they draw no heading, as under `ui:label: false`, rather than dropping it with the heading, reading that rule through `titleDrawsOptionalDataControl()` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `MultiSchemaFieldTemplate` lays out no row for a selector it isn't given, as while an optional `anyOf`/`oneOf` field has no data, rather than an empty one ([#5401](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5401), [#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `FieldHelpTemplate` and `FieldTemplate`'s description render in a `<div>` rather than a `<span>`, which can't hold the blocks `RichHelp` and `RichDescription` render, such as markdown paragraphs. A selector or style aimed at `span#root__help` should aim at `div#root__help` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `FieldTemplate` rendered without a plan draws no label for a field with no label text, which held only a required field's `*` and named its control "star"; a `SelectWidget` no label names is named by the field's label, its placeholder or the select prompt, the multiple select's search input included, through `getControlFallbackName()` ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The date parts of `@rjsf/core`'s `AltDateWidget`, which this theme renders through its `SelectWidget`, are drawn in the error state while the field's errors show, since each part's plan marks it `aria-invalid`, where they never were ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490), [#5542](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5542))
- `SelectWidget`'s single select points its combobox's `aria-activedescendant` at the option cmdk highlights, and its `aria-controls` at the listbox while it is open, so a screen reader announces each option the arrow keys reach while focus stays on the combobox, where it pointed at neither and no highlighted option was announced. Enter chooses the highlighted option, where it closed the listbox without choosing one, so no option could be chosen by keyboard ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `FieldTemplate`'s label puts its plan's label id on a `<span>` around the label's text, as `@rjsf/core`'s does, so a group, a slider or an option selector named through it, such as a required radio group, is named by the text without the `*`; a selector aimed at `label#root_color__label` should aim at `label #root_color__label`. `WrapIfAdditionalTemplate` names an `additionalProperties` entry's key input by the key's label through `aria-label` where `ui:label: false` leaves no label drawn ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `RangeWidget` leaves a range that started empty empty when its form is reset, where Radix restored the minimum it draws the thumb at and the widget committed it. The `name`, `disabled` and `onValueChange` that `ui:options.props` gives it go through its empty-range handling rather than over it, so an empty range named there still submits no value, one disabled there commits nothing when pressed, and one given an `onValueChange` still commits the value a key moves its thumb to. A press cancelled before its release, or a key still held as focus leaves the thumb, commits no minimum, and `ui:autofocus` focuses the thumb only as the range mounts, as `autoFocus` does ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `SelectWidget`'s single select opens its listbox on the option it holds, as a native select does, so Enter keeps that value until the highlight is moved, where it opened on the first option and Enter chose it. With no value it opens on the first option a user can choose, rather than on one an earlier opening left highlighted. It takes the keys of a select-only combobox, as its role leads a screen reader user to expect: Down Arrow, Alt+Down Arrow and Up Arrow open it, Escape closes it, and Space chooses the highlighted option while it is open. Its `aria-activedescendant` follows the highlighted option when the options remount while it is open, and it names its combobox through `getControlAriaLabel()`, so an empty `aria-label` falls back to the field's label, as the multiple select's does. The multiple select's list stays closed as `ui:autofocus` focuses its search input, where it opened over the fields below on mount, and opens on a click or on typing ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `TextareaWidget` keeps its focus ring while its errors show, where its error style removed it, and takes its invalid look from `border-destructive` alone ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- `BaseInputTemplate` keeps `Input`'s focus ring on an invalid input, tinted by its error style, so it still shows a change as it takes keyboard focus now that the input carries `aria-invalid`, whose border otherwise outranks the focus one. The single `SelectWidget` closes on Enter without choosing where the option it opens on, the one it holds, is disabled, moves the highlight from there to that option's neighbour on Down or Up Arrow, on cmdk's Ctrl+N, J, P and K, and on Alt+Arrow where no further group has an option to jump to, and highlights and chooses an option whose value has leading or trailing whitespace, which cmdk trims; the multiple one opens its list on Down Arrow once `ui:autofocus` has focused it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))
- The single `SelectWidget` calls no `onChange` when Enter, Space or a click chooses the option it already holds, as a native select doesn't, and the multiple one closes its list as focus leaves it from a remove button, which opened it ([#5490](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5490))

## @rjsf/snapshot-tests

- The suites no longer mock `getTestIds` themselves. A theme using them adds `@rjsf/snapshot-tests/lib/setup.js` to its vitest `setupFiles`, and `server: { deps: { inline: [/@rjsf\//] } }` so the mock also reaches the `@rjsf/core` it installed from npm
- Added `realValue` select and radio cases with options sharing a `String()`; `@rjsf/validator-ajv8` is now a peer dependency ([#5315](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5315))
- Added `altDateTests()`, which checks the exact accessible name of each part an `AltDateWidget` and `AltDateTimeWidget` render, with the label shown and hidden, in english and through `translateString`, and that each part is described by the field's description and errors ([#5355](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5355), [#5357](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5357))
- Added an `arrayTests` case for an array whose items are named by the `itemLabel` option ([#5474](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5474))
- Added `deprecatedLabelTests()`, which checks that a deprecated field's label carries the Deprecated marker, once, on an untitled property's key, an object's and array's heading, an `anyOf`/`oneOf` field's option selector and option input, and a label the template draws ([#5329](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5329))
- Added `selectedOptionDescriptionTests()`, which checks that a `SelectWidget` shows its selected option's description exactly where it describes the select, under its field's plan with the label shown, hidden by the field's own `ui:label: false` and hidden by the field above, and rendered without a plan with the label shown, hidden by `hideLabel` and hidden by its `uiSchema`'s `ui:label: false`, and that a select rendered without a plan, its label hidden, is named by its caller's `aria-label` and keeps the ids its caller describes it by ([#5505](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5505))
- `altDateTests()` checks that the Now and Clear buttons are enabled by default, disabled when the form is disabled or read-only, and hidden by `hideNowButton` and `hideClearButton`, and that an unset date part reaches `onFocus` and `onBlur` as `undefined` and a set one as its number ([#5378](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5378))
- Added `checkboxLabelTests()`, which checks that a boolean's label and description are drawn once, and its checkbox is named by its label, however `ui:widget` names it: the `checkbox` alias, the `CheckboxWidget` key, that key through `ui:options.widget`, the registered widget passed as a component, a wrapper registered under the `CheckboxWidget` key, and an unregistered wrapper passed as a component. For every spelling but the unregistered wrapper, which takes the theme's `default`, it also checks that the field's plan has the checkbox draw the label, and the description wherever the theme's `descriptionBy` argument says it goes, the checkbox itself or the field's template ([#5389](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5389))
- Added `fallbackDescriptionTests()`, which checks that under `useFallbackUiForUnsupportedType` a field's own `ui:field` naming `FallbackField` or nothing, through `ui:field` or `ui:options.field`, leaves the fallback UI's description drawn once, by the field rather than its value field, where the theme draws it with no `ui:field`, which the theme passes as above or below the type selector ([#5389](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/5389))

## @rjsf/utils

- **BREAKING CHANGE** Removed `defaultProps`-based options merging from `getWidget()`; a widget passed as a component is now returned as-is instead of being wrapped in a cached `MergedWidget`. Apply option defaults inside the widget itself instead (e.g. via destructuring). The now-unused `MergedWidget` property was also removed from the `Widget` type, and the `react-is` dependency was dropped
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** `TranslatableString` and `AdditionalItemsHandling` are now `as const` objects with same-named union types instead of `enum`s, so the source is valid under Node's type stripping. Values (`AdditionalItemsHandling` keeps `0`, `1`, `2`) and `TranslatableString.ArrayItemTitle`-style member access are unchanged; code that used a member as a type must write `typeof TranslatableString.ArrayItemTitle`, and the numeric enum's reverse mapping (`AdditionalItemsHandling[0]`) no longer exists ([#5244](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5244))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** `ObjectFieldTemplatePropertyType` is now generic (`<T, S, F>`, all defaulted like every other RJSF generic type) and its `content` field is typed as `ReactElement<Pick<FieldProps<T, S, F>, 'schema' | 'uiSchema'>>` instead of a bare `ReactElement`. React 19's types default a bare `ReactElement`'s props to `unknown` instead of `any`, which broke themes (e.g. `@rjsf/antd`) that read `.content.props.schema`/`.content.props.uiSchema` directly; the typed version fixes that without widening to `any`. `ObjectFieldTemplateProps['properties']` now threads its own `<T, S, F>` through to `ObjectFieldTemplatePropertyType`. This is only a breaking change if you referenced `ObjectFieldTemplatePropertyType` with explicit (non-default) generics, or relied on `content.props` being typed as `any`
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Removed deprecated exports: the `getUsedFormData()` and `getFieldNames()` functions (no direct replacement), `removeOptionalEmptyObjects()` (use `omitExtraData`, which has the equivalent pruning built in), `toPathSchema()` and the `PathSchema` type (no direct replacement), and the `toPathSchema()` method on `SchemaUtilsType`/`SchemaUtils`. Also removed the `'ui:rootFieldId'` property from the `UiSchema` type; use the `Form.idPrefix` prop instead
- **BREAKING CHANGE** Dropped the `Experimental_`/`experimental_` prefix, now that these features are no longer experimental: the `Experimental_DefaultFormStateBehavior`, `Experimental_ArrayMinItems`, and `Experimental_CustomMergeAllOf` types are now `DefaultFormStateBehavior`, `ArrayMinItems`, and `CustomMergeAllOf`; the `experimental_defaultFormStateBehavior`/`experimental_customMergeAllOf` parameters on `createSchemaUtils()` and the schema utility functions (`getDefaultFormState`, `retrieveSchema`, `isSelect`, `isMultiSelect`, `isFilesArray`, `omitExtraData`, `sanitizeDataForNewSchema`, `getClosestMatchingOption`, `getDisplayLabel`, `getFromSchema`, `findFieldInSchema`, `findSelectedOptionInXxxOf`, and the `SchemaUtilsType`/`SchemaUtils` methods that thread them through) are now `defaultFormStateBehavior`/`customMergeAllOf`
- Added `localTimeToOffsetTime()`/`offsetTimeToLocalTime()` to append/strip a local UTC offset from a bare `time` string and `padTimeSeconds()` to pad a `time` or `date-time` value with `:00` seconds when the native input only provides minute precision, combined into a single `useTimeWidgetProps()` hook — shared by the `TimeWidget`s in `@rjsf/core`, `@rjsf/daisyui`, and `@rjsf/mantine` — that computes a `TimeWidget`'s offset-free display value and a `computeTimeValue()` transform for its `onChange` handler
- Added `getDateTimeLocalValue()`, shared by the `DateTimeWidget`s in `@rjsf/core`, `@rjsf/antd`, `@rjsf/daisyui`, and `@rjsf/mantine`, which reports whether `schema.format` is `iso-date-time` and strips a timezone offset from a stored value's display when it is. `getWidget()`'s `string` widget map also resolves `format: "iso-time"` to `TimeWidget` and `format: "iso-date-time"` to `DateTimeWidget`, same as `time`/`date-time` ([#3930](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/issues/3930))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Added required `MarkdownTemplate` to `TemplatesType` and its `MarkdownTemplateProps` type. The existing `enableMarkdownInDescription` and `enableMarkdownInHelp` options control the registered renderer. ([#5263](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5263))
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** The `InvalidObjectField`, `UnsupportedFieldWithId`, `UnsupportedFieldWithReason`, `UnsupportedFieldWithIdAndReason` and `FilesInfo` translatable strings no longer contain markdown syntax, so they read the same with or without a `MarkdownTemplate` ([#5263](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5263))
- Fixed `mergeObjects()` and `mergeSchemas()` mishandling a key whose left-hand value isn't an object while the right-hand one is. A truthy primitive on the left (`{ a: 5 }` merged with `{ a: { b: 1 } }`) threw `TypeError: Cannot use 'in' operator`, and an array on the left (a tuple `items` merged with a single-schema `items`) produced an object holding the array's indices alongside the right-hand keys. In both cases the right-hand object now replaces the left-hand value, which is what a falsy left-hand value (`false`, `null`, `undefined`) already did. **BREAKING CHANGE:** both functions now require their first argument to be an object, as their signatures have always declared; calling `mergeObjects(undefined, obj)` or `mergeSchemas(undefined, schema)` throws instead of treating the missing argument as `{}`
- **BREAKING CHANGE:** Fixed `ErrorSchema<T>` and `FormValidation<T>` describing the wrong shape for every non-object value. Both mapped over `keyof T`, which is a homomorphic mapped type, and those preserve arrays and primitives rather than describing an error node: `ErrorSchema<string>` resolved to `string`, an array's errors resolved to an array rather than a node keyed by index, and `ErrorSchema<any>` rejected every concrete `ErrorSchema`. A typed form could not express an error on any field. Both types are now built from one shared recursive tree that normalizes the data type first, so leaves, arrays, tuples, optional and nullable properties, atomic objects (`Date`, `File`, ...) and a non-object form root all describe the node that `toErrorSchema()` and `createErrorHandler()` actually build. This is breaking only for code that relied on the old (unusable) shapes; the casts such code needed can now be dropped ([#5278](https://github.com/rjsf-team/react-jsonschema-form/pull/5278))
- **BREAKING CHANGE** `UiSchema<T>` now checks field names against `T` instead of accepting any string key. The type no longer intersects `GenericObjectType`; its per-field entries are a mapped type over the form data, so a misspelled or removed field name is a compile error wherever `T` is known (`FormProps` infers it from `formData`). `UiSchema` with no type argument, or with `any`, is exactly as permissive as before, and the `ui:` namespace stays open at every level, so theme and application directives are unaffected. A union-typed field — what a `oneOf`/`anyOf` property produces — accepts the keys of every branch.
